{"parentUuid":"0e73aa51-43f8-4c57-86bf-1096b030a1ba","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a23eee63b7e067543","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Gdz88KmBoZXF3nWPnsDDre","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→#!/bin/bash\n     2→# POD Platform — Development Server Initialization\n     3→# Starts Next.js app (frontend + API routes on port 3000)\n     4→\n     5→set -e\n     6→\n     7→echo \"Starting POD Platform...\"\n     8→\n     9→# Check if we're in the project directory\n    10→if [ ! -d \"frontend\" ]; then\n    11→  cd \"$(dirname \"$0\")\"\n    12→fi\n    13→\n    14→# Kill any existing processes on port 3000 and clean stale lock files\n    15→lsof -ti:3000 2>/dev/null | xargs kill -9 2>/dev/null || true\n    16→rm -f frontend/.next/dev/lock 2>/dev/null\n    17→\n    18→# Function to kill background processes on exit\n    19→cleanup() {\n    20→  echo \"\""}]},"uuid":"6ddf9ccd-0889-439a-9004-8f92ee389696","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:33:33.110Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"0e73aa51-43f8-4c57-86bf-1096b030a1ba"}

{"parentUuid":"1c8199bf-dd60-4ab2-ae34-60258f9352bb","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a23eee63b7e067543","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EpQuHtCBFksbkmQC4B4xT1","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→\"\"\"\n     2→===============================================================================\n     3→PURE ALGORITHMIC BACKGROUND REMOVAL — RESEARCH & WORKING CODE\n     4→===============================================================================\n     5→For POD (Print-on-Demand) product images.\n     6→NO AI, NO neural networks, NO ONNX, NO ML models.\n     7→Traditional computer vision only.\n     8→\n     9→Target images:\n    10→  - Designs on white backgrounds\n    11→  - Illustrations/vectors on solid color backgrounds\n    12→  - Product photos on studio backgrounds (white/gray)\n    13→\n    14→Output: Clean PNG with transparent background for printing on t-shirts, mugs, etc.\n    15→\n    16→DEPENDENCY SUMMARY:\n    17→  Package                     | Wheel Size   | Installed | Depends On\n    18→  ----------------------------|-------------|-----------|------------\n    19→  Pillow                      | ~5-7 MB     | ~15 MB    | (none)\n    20→  numpy                       | ~8-12 MB    | ~30 MB    | (none)"}]},"uuid":"f0b31205-5a52-4374-a770-d720035359d7","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:33:33.830Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1c8199bf-dd60-4ab2-ae34-60258f9352bb"}

base only\n    24→#   docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.local.yml up -d  # local dev\n    25→#   docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.prod.yml up -d   # production\n    26→\n    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    28→# Reusable fragments (YAML anchors)\n    29→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    30→x-logging: &default-logging\n    31→  driver: json-file\n    32→  options:\n    33→    max-size: \"10m\"\n    34→    max-file: \"3\"\n    35→\n    36→x-security: &default-security\n    37→  cap_drop:\n    38→    - ALL\n    39→\n    40→services:\n    41→  # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    42→  # Frontend — Next.js 16\n    43→  # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    44→  frontend:\n    45→    <<: *default-security\n    46→    build:\n    47→      context: ./frontend\n    48→      dockerfile: Dockerfile\n    49→      args:\n    50→        NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL: ${NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL}"}]},"uuid":"bf233bae-d406-4823-b1d1-7d388466bcdb","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:33:44.395Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"3dd6e9af-0a26-4713-ab68-be72f10c1243"}

{"parentUuid":"f6c030fe-c9e0-4676-9879-13283d1d970b","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a23eee63b7e067543","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01MfrdXHNZcyyspaiEFD5g3T","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# POD AI Store\n     2→\n     3→**Autonomous AI-powered print-on-demand platform with conversational storefront.**\n     4→\n     5→![Node](https://img.shields.io/badge/node-22-339933?logo=node.js&logoColor=white)\n     6→![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/python-3.12-3776AB?logo=python&logoColor=white)\n     7→![Next.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Next.js-16-000000?logo=next.js)\n     8→![License](https://img.shields.io/badge/license-proprietary-red)\n     9→\n    10→---\n    11→\n    12→## Table of Contents\n    13→\n    14→- [Features](#features)\n    15→- [Architecture](#architecture)\n    16→- [Quick Start](#quick-start)\n    17→- [Manual Installation](#manual-installation)\n    18→- [Configuration](#configuration)\n    19→- [Project Structure](#project-structure)\n    20→- [PodClaw Agents](#podclaw-agents)\n    21→- [Deployment](#deployment)\n    22→- [Development](#development)\n    23→- [Troubleshooting](#troubleshooting)\n    24→- [Contributing](#contributing)\n    25→- [License](#license)\n    26→\n    27→---\n    28→\n    29→## Features\n    30→\n    31→- **Conversational Storefront** — The chat IS the store. Three-panel UI with AI-driven product search, design generation, and checkout via 22 tool-augmented interactions.\n    32→- **10 Autonomous Agents (PodClaw)** — Claude-powered agents manage research, design, cataloging, marketing, newsletters, customer support, SEO, finance, quality assurance, and brand compliance on configurable schedules.\n    33→- **AI Design Generation** — On-demand custom designs via fal.ai (FLUX.1) with automatic background removal (rembg sidecar).\n    34→- **Multi-Language** — Full i18n support for English, Spanish, and German with locale-aware routing, emails, and voice input.\n    35→- **Full Ecommerce** — Cart, wishlists, Stripe checkout with tax calculation, Printify fulfillment, order tracking, and return management.\n    36→- **RAG Semantic Search** — Google Gemini embeddings (768-dim) stored in pgvector for intelligent product discovery.\n    37→- **Self-Hostable** — Single `docker compose up` deploys the entire platform with Caddy reverse proxy and automatic HTTPS.\n    38→- **Admin Dashboard** — Real-time agent monitoring, order management, customer analytics, and schedule control.\n    39→\n    40→---\n    41→\n    42→## Architecture\n    43→\n    44→```\n    45→                        ┌──────────────────────┐\n    46→                        │    Caddy (80/443)     │\n    47→                        │  Reverse Proxy + TLS  │\n    48→                        └──────┬───────────────┘\n    49→                               │\n    50→              ┌────────────────┼────────────────┐"}]},"uuid":"20fe7872-d760-4257-9a10-a6da299630ed","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:33:48.682Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f6c030fe-c9e0-4676-9879-13283d1d970b"}

{"parentUuid":"551f063e-35df-470f-b279-eda628b94e80","isSidechain":true,"userType":"external","cwd":"/Users/lr0y/POD-AI-PDR/pod_workspace/project","sessionId":"e8096e0d-c27c-4885-8649-a1485c3b2f75","version":"2.1.71","gitBranch":"master","agentId":"a23eee63b7e067543","slug":"keen-cuddling-rabin","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015r9PssvVqQjuDFziguPYPm","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# POD AI Store — Setup Guide\n     2→\n     3→Complete setup guide for running the Print-on-Demand AI Store platform locally.\n     4→\n     5→## Prerequisites\n     6→\n     7→- **Node.js 22+** (required for both frontend and backend)\n     8→- **Docker Desktop** (for Supabase local instance)\n     9→- **Redis** (via Homebrew, Docker, or cloud service)\n    10→- **Git** (for version control)\n    11→\n    12→## Quick Start (5 minutes)\n    13→\n    14→```bash\n    15→# 1. Install dependencies\n    16→cd project/frontend && npm install\n    17→\n    18→\n    19→# 2. Start Supabase (Docker required)\n    20→cd ..\n    21→supabase start\n    22→\n    23→# This outputs your local credentials — copy them!\n    24→\n    25→# 3. Start Redis\n    26→brew install redis && brew services start redis\n    27→# Or: docker run -d -p 6379:6379 redis:alpine\n    28→\n    29→# 4. Configure environment\n    30→cp backend/.env.example backend/.env\n    31→# Edit backend/.env with your Supabase credentials from step 2\n    32→\n    33→# 5. Start dev servers\n    34→bash init.sh\n    35→\n    36→# ✅ Frontend: http://localhost:3000\n    37→# ✅ Backend: http://localhost:3000\n    38→```\n    39→\n    40→## Detailed Setup\n    41→\n    42→### 1. Database Setup (Supabase)\n    43→\n    44→**Option A: Local Supabase (Recommended for development)**\n    45→\n    46→```bash\n    47→# Install Supabase CLI\n    48→brew install supabase/tap/supabase\n    49→\n    50→# Initialize and start"}]},"uuid":"214d960d-f8a6-42e0-b9ba-6575f556eb61","timestamp":"2026-03-07T18:34:05.932Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"551f063e-35df-470f-b279-eda628b94e80"}

2026-02-23 to 2026-02-24 - Hardening Phase\n    41→\n    42→### Section 10 — Customer Engagement (Features 70)\n    43→\n    44→#### Added\n    45→- **Homepage testimonials section** with real review data and trust signals\n    46→  -
